The station then sees a ship touching a pad it has no clearance for and treats it as a crash, even at near-zero speed and before anything visibly hits. The only protection on the manual takeoff from orbital stations was a 1 cm lift and a 5 cm/s push upward, which the meshes can easily overlap.
